STEP 67 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cat > pkg/kgateway/extensions2/plugins/trafficpolicy/consistent_hash.go <<'EOF'
package trafficpolicy

import (
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	envoyroutev3 "github.com/envoyproxy/go-control-plane/envoy/config/route/v3"
	envoy_type_matcher_v3 "github.com/envoyproxy/go-control-plane/envoy/type/matcher/v3"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/durationpb"

	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/api/v1alpha1/kgateway"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/pluginsdk/ir"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/pluginsdk/policy"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/utils/regexutils"
)

type consistentHashIR struct {
	disable         bool
	headers         []*consistentHashHeaderIR
	cookies         []*consistentHashCookieIR
	queryParameters []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR
	filterState     []*consistentHashFilterStateIR
	sourceIp        *consistentHashSourceIpIR
}

type consistentHashHeaderIR struct {
	headerName   string
	regexRewrite *envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute
	terminal     bool
}

type consistentHashCookieIR struct {
	name       string
	ttl        *time.Duration
	path       string
	attributes []consistentHashCookieAttributeIR
	terminal   bool
}

type consistentHashCookieAttributeIR struct {
	name  string
	value string
}

type consistentHashQueryParameterIR struct {
	name     string
	terminal bool
}

type consistentHashFilterStateIR struct {
	key      string
	terminal bool
}

type consistentHashSourceIpIR struct {
	terminal bool
}

var _ PolicySubIR = &consistentHashIR{}

func (c *consistentHashIR) Equals(other PolicySubIR) bool {
	otherConsistentHash, ok := other.(*consistentHashIR)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	if c == nil && otherConsistentHash == nil {
		return true
	}
	if c == nil || otherConsistentHash == nil {
		return false
	}
	if c.disable != otherConsistentHash.disable {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashHeaders(c.headers, otherConsistentHash.headers) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashCookies(c.cookies, otherConsistentHash.cookies) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashQueryParameters(c.queryParameters, otherConsistentHash.queryParameters) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashFilterStates(c.filterState, otherConsistentHash.filterState) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashSourceIp(c.sourceIp, otherConsistentHash.sourceIp) {
		return false
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashHeaders(a, b []*consistentHashHeaderIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].headerName != b[i].headerName || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
		if !proto.Equal(a[i].regexRewrite, b[i].regexRewrite) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashCookies(a, b []*consistentHashCookieIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].name != b[i].name || a[i].path != b[i].path || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
		if (a[i].ttl == nil) != (b[i].ttl == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i].ttl != nil && *a[i].ttl != *b[i].ttl {
			return false
		}
		if !slices.Equal(a[i].attributes, b[i].attributes) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashQueryParameters(a, b []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].name != b[i].name || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashFilterStates(a, b []*consistentHashFilterStateIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].key != b[i].key || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashSourceIp(a, b *consistentHashSourceIpIR) bool {
	if a == nil || b == nil {
		return a == nil && b == nil
	}
	return a.terminal == b.terminal
}

func (c *consistentHashIR) Validate() error {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	for _, header := range c.headers {
		if header != nil && header.regexRewrite != nil && header.regexRewrite.GetPattern() != nil {
			if err := regexutils.CheckRegexString(header.regexRewrite.GetPattern().GetRegex()); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("invalid regex pattern: %w", err)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func constructConsistentHash(spec kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec, out *trafficPolicySpecIr) error {
	if spec.ConsistentHash == nil {
		return nil
	}

	cfg := spec.ConsistentHash
	ir := &consistentHashIR{
		disable: cfg.Disable,
	}
	if cfg.Disable {
		out.consistentHash = ir
		return nil
	}

	for _, header := range cfg.Headers {
		h := &consistentHashHeaderIR{
			headerName: header.HeaderName,
			terminal:   ptrDeref(header.Terminal),
		}
		if header.RegexRewrite != nil {
			h.regexRewrite = &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute{
				Pattern: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatcher{
					Regex: header.RegexRewrite.Pattern,
				},
				Substitution: header.RegexRewrite.Substitution,
			}
		}
		ir.headers = append(ir.headers, h)
	}

	for _, cookie := range cfg.Cookies {
		c := &consistentHashCookieIR{
			name:     cookie.Name,
			path:     ptrDeref(cookie.Path),
			terminal: ptrDeref(cookie.Terminal),
		}
		if cookie.TTL != nil {
			ttl, err := parseCookieTTL(*cookie.TTL)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			c.ttl = &ttl
		}
		for _, attr := range cookie.Attributes {
			c.attributes = append(c.attributes, consistentHashCookieAttributeIR{
				name:  attr.Name,
				value: attr.Value,
			})
		}
		ir.cookies = append(ir.cookies, c)
	}

	for _, queryParameter := range cfg.QueryParameters {
		ir.queryParameters = append(ir.queryParameters, &consistentHashQueryParameterIR{
			name:     queryParameter.Name,
			terminal: ptrDeref(queryParameter.Terminal),
		})
	}

	for _, filterState := range cfg.FilterState {
		ir.filterState = append(ir.filterState, &consistentHashFilterStateIR{
			key:      filterState.Key,
			terminal: ptrDeref(filterState.Terminal),
		})
	}

	if cfg.SourceIp != nil {
		ir.sourceIp = &consistentHashSourceIpIR{
			terminal: ptrDeref(cfg.SourceIp.Terminal),
		}
	}

	ir.headers = dedupConsistentHashHeaders(ir.headers)
	ir.cookies = dedupConsistentHashCookies(ir.cookies)
	ir.queryParameters = dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(ir.queryParameters)
	ir.filterState = dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(ir.filterState)

	out.consistentHash = ir
	return nil
}

func ptrDeref[T any](p *T) T {
	if p == nil {
		var zero T
		return zero
	}
	return *p
}

func parseCookieTTL(value string) (time.Duration, error) {
	if d, err := time.ParseDuration(value); err == nil {
		return d, nil
	}
	seconds, err := strconv.ParseInt(value, 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid consistent hash cookie ttl %q: expected a Go duration (e.g. \"1h30m\") or integer seconds (e.g. \"3600\")", value)
	}
	return time.Duration(seconds) * time.Second, nil
}

func dedupConsistentHashHeaders(headers []*consistentHashHeaderIR) []*consistentHashHeaderIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(headers))
	out := make([]*consistentHashHeaderIR, 0, len(headers))
	for _, header := range headers {
		if header == nil {
			continue
		}
		key := strings.ToLower(header.headerName)
		if _, ok := seen[key]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[key] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, header)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashCookies(cookies []*consistentHashCookieIR) []*consistentHashCookieIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(cookies))
	out := make([]*consistentHashCookieIR, 0, len(cookies))
	for _, cookie := range cookies {
		if cookie == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[cookie.name]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[cookie.name] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, cookie)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(params []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR) []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(params))
	out := make([]*consistentHashQueryParameterIR, 0, len(params))
	for _, param := range params {
		if param == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[param.name]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[param.name] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, param)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(states []*consistentHashFilterStateIR) []*consistentHashFilterStateIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(states))
	out := make([]*consistentHashFilterStateIR, 0, len(states))
	for _, state := range states {
		if state == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[state.key]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[state.key] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, state)
	}
	return out
}

// applyConsistentHash applies the consistent hash configuration to the Envoy route.
func applyConsistentHash(consistentHash *consistentHashIR, out *envoyroutev3.Route) {
	if consistentHash == nil || out == nil {
		return
	}
	action := out.GetRoute()
	if action == nil {
		return
	}
	if consistentHash.disable {
		action.HashPolicy = nil
		return
	}
	action.HashPolicy = buildHashPolicies(consistentHash)
}

// buildHashPolicies builds the Envoy hash policies in canonical type order:
// headers, cookies, queryParameters, filterState, and finally sourceIp.
func buildHashPolicies(consistentHash *consistentHashIR) []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy {
	if consistentHash == nil || consistentHash.disable {
		return nil
	}

	headers := dedupConsistentHashHeaders(consistentHash.headers)
	cookies := dedupConsistentHashCookies(consistentHash.cookies)
	queryParameters := dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(consistentHash.queryParameters)
	filterStates := dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(consistentHash.filterState)

	var policies []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy

	for _, header := range headers {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Header_{
				Header: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Header{
					HeaderName:   header.headerName,
					RegexRewrite: header.regexRewrite,
				},
			},
			Terminal: header.terminal,
		})
	}

	for _, cookie := range cookies {
		cookiePolicy := &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Cookie{
			Name:       cookie.name,
			Path:       cookie.path,
			Attributes: buildCookieAttributes(cookie.attributes),
		}
		if cookie.ttl != nil {
			cookiePolicy.Ttl = durationpb.New(*cookie.ttl)
		}
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Cookie_{
				Cookie: cookiePolicy,
			},
			Terminal: cookie.terminal,
		})
	}

	for _, queryParameter := range queryParameters {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_QueryParameter_{
				QueryParameter: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_QueryParameter{
					Name: queryParameter.name,
				},
			},
			Terminal: queryParameter.terminal,
		})
	}

	for _, filterState := range filterStates {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_FilterState_{
				FilterState: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_FilterState{
					Key: filterState.key,
				},
			},
			Terminal: filterState.terminal,
		})
	}

	if consistentHash.sourceIp != nil {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties_{
				ConnectionProperties: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties{
					SourceIp: true,
				},
			},
			Terminal: consistentHash.sourceIp.terminal,
		})
	}

	// If no hash policies were specified, default to a single sourceIp hash policy.
	if len(policies) == 0 {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties_{
				ConnectionProperties: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties{
					SourceIp: true,
				},
			},
		})
	}

	return policies
}

func buildCookieAttributes(attributes []consistentHashCookieAttributeIR) []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute {
	out := make([]*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute, 0, len(attributes))
	for _, attribute := range attributes {
		out = append(out, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute{
			Name:  attribute.name,
			Value: attribute.value,
		})
	}
	return out
}

func cloneConsistentHash(c *consistentHashIR) *consistentHashIR {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := &consistentHashIR{disable: c.disable}
	for _, header := range c.headers {
		out.headers = append(out.headers, cloneConsistentHashHeader(header))
	}
	for _, cookie := range c.cookies {
		out.cookies = append(out.cookies, cloneConsistentHashCookie(cookie))
	}
	for _, queryParameter := range c.queryParameters {
		out.queryParameters = append(out.queryParameters, &consistentHashQueryParameterIR{
			name:     queryParameter.name,
			terminal: queryParameter.terminal,
		})
	}
	for _, filterState := range c.filterState {
		out.filterState = append(out.filterState, &consistentHashFilterStateIR{
			key:      filterState.key,
			terminal: filterState.terminal,
		})
	}
	if c.sourceIp != nil {
		out.sourceIp = &consistentHashSourceIpIR{terminal: c.sourceIp.terminal}
	}
	return out
}

func cloneConsistentHashHeader(h *consistentHashHeaderIR) *consistentHashHeaderIR {
	if h == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := &consistentHashHeaderIR{
		headerName: h.headerName,
		terminal:   h.terminal,
	}
	if h.regexRewrite != nil {
		out.regexRewrite = proto.Clone(h.regexRewrite).(*envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute)
	}
	return out
}

func cloneConsistentHashCookie(c *consistentHashCookieIR) *consistentHashCookieIR {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := &consistentHashCookieIR{
		name:       c.name,
		path:       c.path,
		terminal:   c.terminal,
		attributes: slices.Clone(c.attributes),
	}
	if c.ttl != nil {
		ttl := *c.ttl
		out.ttl = &ttl
	}
	return out
}

// mergeConsistentHashIRs merges two consistent hash IRs, preferring the primary policy.
// Array fields are unioned with primary entries first and deduplicated by key, while
// scalar fields (disable and sourceIp) retain the primary policy's value even when unset.
func mergeConsistentHashIRs(primary, secondary *consistentHashIR) *consistentHashIR {
	if primary == nil {
		return cloneConsistentHash(secondary)
	}
	if primary.disable {
		return &consistentHashIR{disable: true}
	}
	if secondary == nil {
		return cloneConsistentHash(primary)
	}

	merged := cloneConsistentHash(primary)
	merged.headers = dedupConsistentHashHeaders(slices.Concat(merged.headers, secondary.headers...))
	merged.cookies = dedupConsistentHashCookies(slices.Concat(merged.cookies, secondary.cookies...))
	merged.queryParameters = dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(slices.Concat(merged.queryParameters, secondary.queryParameters...))
	merged.filterState = dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(slices.Concat(merged.filterState, secondary.filterState...))
	// sourceIp is a scalar and retains the primary policy's value even when unset.
	return merged
}

func mergeConsistentHash(
	p1, p2 *TrafficPolicy,
	p2Ref *ir.AttachedPolicyRef,
	p2MergeOrigins ir.MergeOrigins,
	opts policy.MergeOptions,
	mergeOrigins ir.MergeOrigins,
	tpOpts TrafficPolicyMergeOpts,
) {
	if tpOpts.ConsistentHash != "" {
		// this is merging 2 policies at the same hierarchical level (no parent-child relationship),
		// so use tpOpts since it overrides the default merge strategy
		opts.Strategy = policy.ToInternalMergeStrategy(tpOpts.ConsistentHash)
	}

	if !policy.IsMergeable(p1.spec.consistentHash, p2.spec.consistentHash, opts) {
		return
	}

	switch opts.Strategy {
	case policy.AugmentedDeepMerge, policy.OverridableDeepMerge:
		if p1.spec.consistentHash == nil {
			p1.spec.consistentHash = cloneConsistentHash(p2.spec.consistentHash)
			mergeOrigins.SetOne("consistentHash", p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins)
			return
		}
		if p2.spec.consistentHash == nil {
			return
		}

		primary, secondary := p1.spec.consistentHash, p2.spec.consistentHash
		if opts.Strategy == policy.OverridableDeepMerge {
			primary, secondary = p2.spec.consistentHash, p1.spec.consistentHash
		}

		if primary.disable {
			// A higher-priority disable suppresses all hash policies from lower-priority policies.
			p1.spec.consistentHash = &consistentHashIR{disable: true}
			return
		}

		p1.spec.consistentHash = mergeConsistentHashIRs(primary, secondary)
		mergeOrigins.Append("consistentHash", p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins)

	default:
		accessor := fieldAccessor[consistentHashIR]{
			Get: func(spec *trafficPolicySpecIr) *consistentHashIR { return spec.consistentHash },
			Set: func(spec *trafficPolicySpecIr, val *consistentHashIR) { spec.consistentHash = val },
		}
		defaultMerge(p1, p2, p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins, opts, mergeOrigins, accessor, "consistentHash")
	}
}

package trafficpolicy

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	envoyroutev3 "github.com/envoyproxy/go-control-plane/envoy/config/route/v3"
	envoy_type_matcher_v3 "github.com/envoyproxy/go-control-plane/envoy/type/matcher/v3"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/durationpb"

	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/api/v1alpha1/kgateway"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/pluginsdk/ir"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/pluginsdk/policy"
)

func TestConstructConsistentHash(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("nil config", func(t *testing.T) {
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		require.NoError(t, constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{}, out))
		assert.Nil(t, out.consistentHash)
	})

	t.Run("empty config", func(t *testing.T) {
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		require.NoError(t, constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{
			ConsistentHash: &kgateway.ConsistentHashConfig{},
		}, out))
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash)
		assert.False(t, out.consistentHash.disable)
		assert.Empty(t, out.consistentHash.headers)
		assert.Empty(t, out.consistentHash.cookies)
		assert.Nil(t, out.consistentHash.sourceIp)
	})

	t.Run("full config", func(t *testing.T) {
		terminal := true
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		require.NoError(t, constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{
			ConsistentHash: &kgateway.ConsistentHashConfig{
				Headers: []kgateway.ConsistentHashHeader{
					{
						HeaderName: "x-user",
						RegexRewrite: &kgateway.ConsistentHashRegexRewrite{
							Pattern:      "^(.*)-suffix$",
							Substitution: "$1",
						},
						Terminal: &terminal,
					},
				},
				Cookies: []kgateway.ConsistentHashCookie{
					{
						Name:  "session",
						TTL:   strPtr("1h30m"),
						Path:  strPtr("/"),
						Terminal: &terminal,
					},
				},
				QueryParameters: []kgateway.ConsistentHashQueryParameter{{Name: "user_id"}},
				FilterState:     []kgateway.ConsistentHashFilterState{{Key: "state_key"}},
				SourceIp:        &kgateway.ConsistentHashSourceIp{Terminal: &terminal},
			},
		}, out))
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash)
		require.Len(t, out.consistentHash.headers, 1)
		assert.Equal(t, "x-user", out.consistentHash.headers[0].headerName)
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash.headers[0].regexRewrite)
		assert.Equal(t, "^(.*)-suffix$", out.consistentHash.headers[0].regexRewrite.GetPattern().GetRegex())
		require.Len(t, out.consistentHash.cookies, 1)
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash.cookies[0].ttl)
		assert.Equal(t, 90*time.Minute, *out.consistentHash.cookies[0].ttl)
		require.Len(t, out.consistentHash.queryParameters, 1)
		require.Len(t, out.consistentHash.filterState, 1)
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash.sourceIp)
	})

	t.Run("integer seconds ttl", func(t *testing.T) {
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		require.NoError(t, constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{
			ConsistentHash: &kgateway.ConsistentHashConfig{
				Cookies: []kgateway.ConsistentHashCookie{{Name: "session", TTL: strPtr("3600")}},
			},
		}, out))
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash.cookies[0].ttl)
		assert.Equal(t, time.Hour, *out.consistentHash.cookies[0].ttl)
	})

	t.Run("invalid ttl", func(t *testing.T) {
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		err := constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{
			ConsistentHash: &kgateway.ConsistentHashConfig{
				Cookies: []kgateway.ConsistentHashCookie{{Name: "session", TTL: strPtr("not-a-duration")}},
			},
		}, out)
		require.Error(t, err)
	})

	t.Run("disable", func(t *testing.T) {
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		require.NoError(t, constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{
			ConsistentHash: &kgateway.ConsistentHashConfig{
				Disable: true,
				Headers: []kgateway.ConsistentHashHeader{{HeaderName: "x-user"}},
			},
		}, out))
		require.NotNil(t, out.consistentHash)
		assert.True(t, out.consistentHash.disable)
		assert.Empty(t, out.consistentHash.headers)
	})

	t.Run("deduplicates headers case-insensitively preserving first casing", func(t *testing.T) {
		out := &trafficPolicySpecIr{}
		require.NoError(t, constructConsistentHash(kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec{
			ConsistentHash: &kgateway.ConsistentHashConfig{
				Headers: []kgateway.ConsistentHashHeader{
					{HeaderName: "X-User"},
					{HeaderName: "x-user"},
				},
			},
		}, out))
		require.Len(t, out.consistentHash.headers, 1)
		assert.Equal(t, "X-User", out.consistentHash.headers[0].headerName)
	})
}

func TestBuildHashPolicies(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("empty config defaults to sourceIp", func(t *testing.T) {
		policies := buildHashPolicies(&consistentHashIR{})
		require.Len(t, policies, 1)
		assert.True(t, policies[0].GetConnectionProperties().GetSourceIp())
		assert.False(t, policies[0].GetTerminal())
	})

	t.Run("disable produces no policies", func(t *testing.T) {
		assert.Nil(t, buildHashPolicies(&consistentHashIR{disable: true}))
	})

	t.Run("canonical order", func(t *testing.T) {
		ch := &consistentHashIR{
			sourceIp:        &consistentHashSourceIpIR{},
			headers:         []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
			cookies:         []*consistentHashCookieIR{{name: "session"}},
			queryParameters: []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR{{name: "user_id"}},
			filterState:     []*consistentHashFilterStateIR{{key: "state_key"}},
		}
		policies := buildHashPolicies(ch)
		require.Len(t, policies, 5)
		assert.NotNil(t, policies[0].GetHeader())
		assert.NotNil(t, policies[1].GetCookie())
		assert.NotNil(t, policies[2].GetQueryParameter())
		assert.NotNil(t, policies[3].GetFilterState())
		assert.NotNil(t, policies[4].GetConnectionProperties())
	})

	t.Run("regex rewrite and cookie attributes", func(t *testing.T) {
		ch := &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{
				headerName: "x-user",
				regexRewrite: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute{
					Pattern:      &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatcher{Regex: "^(.*)-suffix$"},
					Substitution: "$1",
				},
				terminal: true,
			}},
			cookies: []*consistentHashCookieIR{{
				name: "session",
				ttl:  durationPtr(time.Hour),
				path: "/",
				attributes: []consistentHashCookieAttributeIR{
					{name: "SameSite", value: "Strict"},
					{name: "Secure"},
				},
			}},
		}
		policies := buildHashPolicies(ch)
		require.Len(t, policies, 2)
		header := policies[0].GetHeader()
		require.NotNil(t, header)
		assert.Equal(t, "^(.*)-suffix$", header.GetRegexRewrite().GetPattern().GetRegex())
		assert.True(t, policies[0].GetTerminal())
		cookie := policies[1].GetCookie()
		require.NotNil(t, cookie)
		assert.Equal(t, durationpb.New(time.Hour), cookie.GetTtl())
		assert.Equal(t, "/", cookie.GetPath())
		require.Len(t, cookie.GetAttributes(), 2)
		assert.Equal(t, "SameSite", cookie.GetAttributes()[0].GetName())
		assert.Equal(t, "Strict", cookie.GetAttributes()[0].GetValue())
		assert.Equal(t, "Secure", cookie.GetAttributes()[1].GetName())
	})
}

func TestApplyConsistentHash(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("nil config does nothing", func(t *testing.T) {
		route := &envoyroutev3.Route{Action: &envoyroutev3.Route_Route{Route: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction{}}}
		applyConsistentHash(nil, route)
		assert.Empty(t, route.GetRoute().GetHashPolicy())
	})

	t.Run("applies hash policies", func(t *testing.T) {
		route := &envoyroutev3.Route{Action: &envoyroutev3.Route_Route{Route: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction{}}}
		applyConsistentHash(&consistentHashIR{headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}}}, route)
		require.Len(t, route.GetRoute().GetHashPolicy(), 1)
		assert.Equal(t, "x-user", route.GetRoute().GetHashPolicy()[0].GetHeader().GetHeaderName())
	})

	t.Run("disable clears existing hash policies", func(t *testing.T) {
		route := &envoyroutev3.Route{
			Action: &envoyroutev3.Route_Route{
				Route: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction{
					HashPolicy: []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{{
						PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Header_{
							Header: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Header{HeaderName: "x-user"},
						},
					}},
				},
			},
		}
		applyConsistentHash(&consistentHashIR{disable: true}, route)
		assert.Empty(t, route.GetRoute().GetHashPolicy())
	})
}

func TestMergeConsistentHash(t *testing.T) {
	ref := &ir.AttachedPolicyRef{Name: "p2"}

	t.Run("deep merge unions arrays with higher priority first", func(t *testing.T) {
		p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
			sourceIp: &consistentHashSourceIpIR{terminal: true},
		}}}
		p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}, {headerName: "X-Account"}},
			cookies: []*consistentHashCookieIR{{name: "session"}},
		}}}
		origins := ir.MergeOrigins{}
		mergeConsistentHash(p1, p2, ref, nil, policy.MergeOptions{Strategy: policy.AugmentedDeepMerge}, origins, TrafficPolicyMergeOpts{})
		require.NotNil(t, p1.spec.consistentHash)
		assert.Equal(t, []string{"x-user", "X-Account"}, headersNames(p1.spec.consistentHash.headers))
		require.Len(t, p1.spec.consistentHash.cookies, 1)
		// sourceIp retains higher-priority value even when lower priority leaves it unset.
		require.NotNil(t, p1.spec.consistentHash.sourceIp)
		assert.True(t, p1.spec.consistentHash.sourceIp.terminal)
		_, ok := origins["consistentHash"]
		assert.True(t, ok)
	})

	t.Run("overridable deep merge prefers p2", func(t *testing.T) {
		p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
		}}}
		p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "X-Account"}},
		}}}
		origins := ir.MergeOrigins{}
		mergeConsistentHash(p1, p2, ref, nil, policy.MergeOptions{Strategy: policy.OverridableDeepMerge}, origins, TrafficPolicyMergeOpts{})
		require.NotNil(t, p1.spec.consistentHash)
		assert.Equal(t, []string{"X-Account", "x-user"}, headersNames(p1.spec.consistentHash.headers))
	})

	t.Run("shallow merge prefers p1", func(t *testing.T) {
		p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
		}}}
		p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-account"}},
		}}}
		origins := ir.MergeOrigins{}
		mergeConsistentHash(p1, p2, ref, nil, policy.MergeOptions{Strategy: policy.AugmentedShallowMerge}, origins, TrafficPolicyMergeOpts{})
		assert.Equal(t, []string{"x-user"}, headersNames(p1.spec.consistentHash.headers))
	})

	t.Run("higher priority disable suppresses lower priority", func(t *testing.T) {
		p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{disable: true}}}
		p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
		}}}
		origins := ir.MergeOrigins{}
		mergeConsistentHash(p1, p2, ref, nil, policy.MergeOptions{Strategy: policy.AugmentedDeepMerge}, origins, TrafficPolicyMergeOpts{})
		require.NotNil(t, p1.spec.consistentHash)
		assert.True(t, p1.spec.consistentHash.disable)
		assert.Empty(t, p1.spec.consistentHash.headers)
		assert.Empty(t, origins["consistentHash"])
	})

	t.Run("merge settings deep merge", func(t *testing.T) {
		p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
		}}}
		p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
			headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-account"}},
		}}}
		origins := ir.MergeOrigins{}
		mergeConsistentHash(p1, p2, ref, nil, policy.MergeOptions{Strategy: policy.AugmentedShallowMerge}, origins, TrafficPolicyMergeOpts{ConsistentHash: "DeepMerge"})
		assert.Equal(t, []string{"x-user", "x-account"}, headersNames(p1.spec.consistentHash.headers))
	})
}

func headersNames(headers []*consistentHashHeaderIR) []string {
	out := make([]string, 0, len(headers))
	for _, h := range headers {
		out = append(out, h.headerName)
	}
	return out
}

func mergePolicies(pass *TranslationPass, policies []ir.PolicyAtt) ([]ir.PolicyAtt, ir.MergeOrigins) {
	if pass.MergePolicies != nil {
		mergedPolicy := pass.MergePolicies(policies)
		merged := [1]ir.PolicyAtt{mergedPolicy}
		return merged[:], mergedPolicy.MergeOrigins
	}

	return policies, nil
}

func (h *httpRouteConfigurationTranslator) runBackendPolicies(in ir.HttpBackend, pCtx *ir.RouteBackendContext) error {
	var errs []error
	for _, gk := range in.AttachedPolicies.ApplyOrderedGroupKinds() {
		pols := in.AttachedPolicies.Policies[gk]

			// TODO: should never happen, log error and report condition
			continue
		}
		reportPolicyAcceptanceStatus(h.reporter, h.listener.PolicyAncestorRef, pols...)
		policies, _ := mergePolicies(pass, pols)
		for _, pol := range policies {
			// Policy on extension ref
			err := pass.ApplyForRouteBackend(pol.PolicyIr, pCtx)

		}
		policiesByLabel := p.fetchByTargetRefLabels(kctx, refIndexKeyByNamespace, onlyBackends, targetLabels)
		policies = append(policies, policiesByLabel...)

		// Check if policies defined in the global policy namespace target this ref.
		// `targetRef.Namespace != p.globalPolicyNamespace` ensures we avoid a duplicate lookup as done
		// above when targetRef.Namespace is the same as globalPolicyNamespace
		if p.globalPolicyNamespace != "" && targetRef.Namespace != p.globalPolicyNamespace {
			refIndexKeyByNamespace.Namespace = p.globalPolicyNamespace
			globalPolicies := p.fetchByTargetRefLabels(kctx, refIndexKeyByNamespace, onlyBackends, targetLabels)
			policies = append(policies, globalPolicies...)
		}
	}

	for _, p := range policies {
		ret = append(ret, ir.PolicyAtt{
			Generation: p.Policy.GetGeneration(),
			GroupKind:  p.GetGroupKind(),
			PolicyIr:   p.PolicyIR,
			PolicyRef: &ir.AttachedPolicyRef{
				Group:       p.Group,
				Kind:        p.Kind,
				Name:        p.Name,
				Namespace:   p.Namespace,
				SectionName: sectionName,

			PrecedenceWeight: p.PrecedenceWeight,
			Errors:           p.Errors,
		})
	}

	slices.SortFunc(ret, func(a, b ir.PolicyAtt) int {
		// Sort policies by their PrecedenceWeight for the same kind if the weights are different,
		// otherwise sort by creation time
		if a.GroupKind == b.GroupKind {
			if a.PrecedenceWeight > b.PrecedenceWeight {
				return -1
			} else if a.PrecedenceWeight < b.PrecedenceWeight {
				return 1
			}
		}
		return a.PolicyIr.CreationTime().Compare(b.PolicyIr.CreationTime())
	})
	return ret
}

// handlePerRoutePolicies handles policies that are meant to be processed at the route level
func (p *trafficPolicyPluginGwPass) handlePerRoutePolicies(
	spec trafficPolicySpecIr,
	out *envoyroutev3.Route,
) {
	// A parent route rule with a delegated backend will not have RouteAction set.
	// Routes with redirect/direct response also do not have RouteAction.
	if out.GetRoute() == nil {
		return
	}

	action := out.GetRoute()

	if spec.autoHostRewrite != nil && spec.autoHostRewrite.enabled != nil && spec.autoHostRewrite.enabled.GetValue() {
		// Only apply TrafficPolicy's AutoHostRewrite if built-in policy's AutoHostRewrite is not already set
		if action.GetHostRewriteSpecifier() == nil {
			action.HostRewriteSpecifier = &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_AutoHostRewrite{
				AutoHostRewrite: spec.autoHostRewrite.enabled,
			}
		}
	}

	// Apply consistent hash configuration
	applyConsistentHash(spec.consistentHash, out)

	if spec.timeouts != nil {
		action.IdleTimeout = spec.timeouts.routeStreamIdleTimeout
		// Only set the route timeout if it is not already set, which implies that it was
		// set by the builtin HTTPRouteTimeouts policy
		if action.GetTimeout() == nil {
			action.Timeout = spec.timeouts.routeTimeout
		}
	}

	// Only set the retry policy if it is not already set, which implies that it was
	// set by the builtin HTTPRouteRetry policy
	if action.GetRetryPolicy() == nil && spec.retry != nil {
		action.RetryPolicy = spec.retry.policy
	}

	// Apply URL rewrite configuration
	applyURLRewrite(spec.urlRewrite, out)
}

// handlePerVHostPolicies handles policies that are meant to be processed at the vhost level
func (p *trafficPolicyPluginGwPass) handlePerVHostPolicies(
	spec trafficPolicySpecIr,
	out *envoyroutev3.VirtualHost,
) {

package trafficpolicy

import (
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	envoyroutev3 "github.com/envoyproxy/go-control-plane/envoy/config/route/v3"
	envoy_type_matcher_v3 "github.com/envoyproxy/go-control-plane/envoy/type/matcher/v3"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/durationpb"

	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/api/v1alpha1/kgateway"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/pluginsdk/ir"
	"github.com/kgateway-dev/kgateway/v2/pkg/pluginsdk/policy"

type consistentHashIR struct {
	disable         bool
	headers         []*consistentHashHeaderIR
	cookies         []*consistentHashCookieIR
	queryParameters []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR
	filterState     []*consistentHashFilterStateIR
	sourceIp        *consistentHashSourceIpIR
}

type consistentHashHeaderIR struct {
	headerName   string
	regexRewrite *envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute
	terminal     bool
}

type consistentHashCookieIR struct {
	name       string
	ttl        *time.Duration
	path       string
	attributes []consistentHashCookieAttributeIR
	terminal   bool
}

type consistentHashCookieAttributeIR struct {
	name  string
	value string
}

type consistentHashQueryParameterIR struct {
	name     string
	terminal bool
}

type consistentHashFilterStateIR struct {
	key      string
	terminal bool
}

type consistentHashSourceIpIR struct {
	terminal bool
}

var _ PolicySubIR = &consistentHashIR{}

func (c *consistentHashIR) Equals(other PolicySubIR) bool {
	otherConsistentHash, ok := other.(*consistentHashIR)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	if c == nil && otherConsistentHash == nil {
		return true
	}
	if c == nil || otherConsistentHash == nil {
		return false
	}
	if c.disable != otherConsistentHash.disable {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashHeaders(c.headers, otherConsistentHash.headers) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashCookies(c.cookies, otherConsistentHash.cookies) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashQueryParameters(c.queryParameters, otherConsistentHash.queryParameters) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashFilterStates(c.filterState, otherConsistentHash.filterState) {
		return false
	}
	if !equalConsistentHashSourceIp(c.sourceIp, otherConsistentHash.sourceIp) {
		return false
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashHeaders(a, b []*consistentHashHeaderIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].headerName != b[i].headerName || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
		if !proto.Equal(a[i].regexRewrite, b[i].regexRewrite) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashCookies(a, b []*consistentHashCookieIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].name != b[i].name || a[i].path != b[i].path || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
		if (a[i].ttl == nil) != (b[i].ttl == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i].ttl != nil && *a[i].ttl != *b[i].ttl {
			return false
		}
		if !slices.Equal(a[i].attributes, b[i].attributes) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashQueryParameters(a, b []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].name != b[i].name || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashFilterStates(a, b []*consistentHashFilterStateIR) bool {
	if len(a) != len(b) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if (a[i] == nil) != (b[i] == nil) {
			return false
		}
		if a[i] == nil {
			continue
		}
		if a[i].key != b[i].key || a[i].terminal != b[i].terminal {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func equalConsistentHashSourceIp(a, b *consistentHashSourceIpIR) bool {
	if a == nil || b == nil {
		return a == nil && b == nil
	}
	return a.terminal == b.terminal
}

func (c *consistentHashIR) Validate() error {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	for _, header := range c.headers {
		if header != nil && header.regexRewrite != nil && header.regexRewrite.GetPattern() != nil {
			if err := regexutils.CheckRegexString(header.regexRewrite.GetPattern().GetRegex()); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("invalid regex pattern: %w", err)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func constructConsistentHash(spec kgateway.TrafficPolicySpec, out *trafficPolicySpecIr) error {
	if spec.ConsistentHash == nil {
		return nil
	}

	cfg := spec.ConsistentHash
	ir := &consistentHashIR{
		disable: cfg.Disable,
	}
	if cfg.Disable {
		out.consistentHash = ir
		return nil
	}

	for _, header := range cfg.Headers {
		h := &consistentHashHeaderIR{
			headerName: header.HeaderName,
			terminal:   ptrDeref(header.Terminal),
		}
		if header.RegexRewrite != nil {
			h.regexRewrite = &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute{
				Pattern: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatcher{
					Regex: header.RegexRewrite.Pattern,
				},
				Substitution: header.RegexRewrite.Substitution,
			}
		}
		ir.headers = append(ir.headers, h)
	}

	for _, cookie := range cfg.Cookies {
		c := &consistentHashCookieIR{
			name:     cookie.Name,
			path:     ptrDeref(cookie.Path),
			terminal: ptrDeref(cookie.Terminal),
		}
		if cookie.TTL != nil {
			ttl, err := parseCookieTTL(*cookie.TTL)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			c.ttl = &ttl
		}
		for _, attr := range cookie.Attributes {
			c.attributes = append(c.attributes, consistentHashCookieAttributeIR{
				name:  attr.Name,
				value: attr.Value,
			})
		}
		ir.cookies = append(ir.cookies, c)
	}

	for _, queryParameter := range cfg.QueryParameters {
		ir.queryParameters = append(ir.queryParameters, &consistentHashQueryParameterIR{
			name:     queryParameter.Name,
			terminal: ptrDeref(queryParameter.Terminal),
		})
	}

	for _, filterState := range cfg.FilterState {
		ir.filterState = append(ir.filterState, &consistentHashFilterStateIR{
			key:      filterState.Key,
			terminal: ptrDeref(filterState.Terminal),
		})
	}

	if cfg.SourceIp != nil {
		ir.sourceIp = &consistentHashSourceIpIR{
			terminal: ptrDeref(cfg.SourceIp.Terminal),
		}
	}

	ir.headers = dedupConsistentHashHeaders(ir.headers)
	ir.cookies = dedupConsistentHashCookies(ir.cookies)
	ir.queryParameters = dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(ir.queryParameters)
	ir.filterState = dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(ir.filterState)

	out.consistentHash = ir
	return nil
}

func ptrDeref[T any](p *T) T {
	if p == nil {
		var zero T
		return zero
	}
	return *p
}

func parseCookieTTL(value string) (time.Duration, error) {
	if d, err := time.ParseDuration(value); err == nil {
		return d, nil
	}
	seconds, err := strconv.ParseInt(value, 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid consistent hash cookie ttl %q: expected a Go duration (e.g. \"1h30m\") or integer seconds (e.g. \"3600\")", value)
	}
	return time.Duration(seconds) * time.Second, nil
}

func dedupConsistentHashHeaders(headers []*consistentHashHeaderIR) []*consistentHashHeaderIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(headers))
	out := make([]*consistentHashHeaderIR, 0, len(headers))
	for _, header := range headers {
		if header == nil {
			continue
		}
		key := strings.ToLower(header.headerName)
		if _, ok := seen[key]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[key] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, header)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashCookies(cookies []*consistentHashCookieIR) []*consistentHashCookieIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(cookies))
	out := make([]*consistentHashCookieIR, 0, len(cookies))
	for _, cookie := range cookies {
		if cookie == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[cookie.name]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[cookie.name] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, cookie)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(params []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR) []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(params))
	out := make([]*consistentHashQueryParameterIR, 0, len(params))
	for _, param := range params {
		if param == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[param.name]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[param.name] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, param)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(states []*consistentHashFilterStateIR) []*consistentHashFilterStateIR {
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(states))
	out := make([]*consistentHashFilterStateIR, 0, len(states))
	for _, state := range states {
		if state == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[state.key]; ok {
			continue
		}

OUTPUT:
		}
		seen[state.key] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, state)
	}
	return out
}

// applyConsistentHash applies the consistent hash configuration to the Envoy route.
func applyConsistentHash(consistentHash *consistentHashIR, out *envoyroutev3.Route) {
	if consistentHash == nil || out == nil {
		return
	}
	action := out.GetRoute()
	if action == nil {
		return
	}
	if consistentHash.disable {
		action.HashPolicy = nil
		return
	}
	action.HashPolicy = buildHashPolicies(consistentHash)
}

// buildHashPolicies builds the Envoy hash policies in canonical type order:
// headers, cookies, queryParameters, filterState, and finally sourceIp.
func buildHashPolicies(consistentHash *consistentHashIR) []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy {
	if consistentHash == nil || consistentHash.disable {
		return nil
	}

	headers := dedupConsistentHashHeaders(consistentHash.headers)
	cookies := dedupConsistentHashCookies(consistentHash.cookies)
	queryParameters := dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(consistentHash.queryParameters)
	filterStates := dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(consistentHash.filterState)

	var policies []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy

	for _, header := range headers {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Header_{
				Header: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Header{
					HeaderName:   header.headerName,
					RegexRewrite: header.regexRewrite,
				},
			},
			Terminal: header.terminal,
		})
	}

	for _, cookie := range cookies {
		cookiePolicy := &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Cookie{
			Name:       cookie.name,
			Path:       cookie.path,
			Attributes: buildCookieAttributes(cookie.attributes),
		}
		if cookie.ttl != nil {
			cookiePolicy.Ttl = durationpb.New(*cookie.ttl)
		}
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_Cookie_{
				Cookie: cookiePolicy,
			},
			Terminal: cookie.terminal,
		})
	}

	for _, queryParameter := range queryParameters {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_QueryParameter_{
				QueryParameter: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_QueryParameter{
					Name: queryParameter.name,
				},
			},
			Terminal: queryParameter.terminal,
		})
	}

	for _, filterState := range filterStates {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_FilterState_{
				FilterState: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_FilterState{
					Key: filterState.key,
				},
			},
			Terminal: filterState.terminal,
		})
	}

	if consistentHash.sourceIp != nil {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties_{
				ConnectionProperties: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties{
					SourceIp: true,
				},
			},
			Terminal: consistentHash.sourceIp.terminal,
		})
	}

	// If no hash policies were specified, default to a single sourceIp hash policy.
	if len(policies) == 0 {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties_{
				ConnectionProperties: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties{
					SourceIp: true,
				},
			},
		})
	}

	return policies
}

func buildCookieAttributes(attributes []consistentHashCookieAttributeIR) []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute {
	out := make([]*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute, 0, len(attributes))
	for _, attribute := range attributes {
		out = append(out, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute{
			Name:  attribute.name,
			Value: attribute.value,
		})
	}
	return out
}

func cloneConsistentHash(c *consistentHashIR) *consistentHashIR {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := &consistentHashIR{disable: c.disable}
	for _, header := range c.headers {
		out.headers = append(out.headers, cloneConsistentHashHeader(header))
	}
	for _, cookie := range c.cookies {
		out.cookies = append(out.cookies, cloneConsistentHashCookie(cookie))
	}
	for _, queryParameter := range c.queryParameters {
		out.queryParameters = append(out.queryParameters, &consistentHashQueryParameterIR{
			name:     queryParameter.name,
			terminal: queryParameter.terminal,
		})
	}
	for _, filterState := range c.filterState {
		out.filterState = append(out.filterState, &consistentHashFilterStateIR{
			key:      filterState.key,
			terminal: filterState.terminal,
		})
	}
	if c.sourceIp != nil {
		out.sourceIp = &consistentHashSourceIpIR{terminal: c.sourceIp.terminal}
	}
	return out
}

func cloneConsistentHashHeader(h *consistentHashHeaderIR) *consistentHashHeaderIR {
	if h == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := &consistentHashHeaderIR{
		headerName: h.headerName,
		terminal:   h.terminal,
	}
	if h.regexRewrite != nil {
		out.regexRewrite = proto.Clone(h.regexRewrite).(*envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute)
	}
	return out
}

func cloneConsistentHashCookie(c *consistentHashCookieIR) *consistentHashCookieIR {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := &consistentHashCookieIR{
		name:       c.name,
		path:       c.path,
		terminal:   c.terminal,
		attributes: slices.Clone(c.attributes),
	}
	if c.ttl != nil {
		ttl := *c.ttl
		out.ttl = &ttl
	}
	return out
}

// mergeConsistentHashIRs merges two consistent hash IRs, preferring the primary policy.
// Array fields are unioned with primary entries first and deduplicated by key, while
// scalar fields (disable and sourceIp) retain the primary policy's value even when unset.
func mergeConsistentHashIRs(primary, secondary *consistentHashIR) *consistentHashIR {
	if primary == nil {
		return cloneConsistentHash(secondary)
	}
	if primary.disable {
		return &consistentHashIR{disable: true}
	}
	if secondary == nil {
		return cloneConsistentHash(primary)
	}

	merged := cloneConsistentHash(primary)
	merged.headers = dedupConsistentHashHeaders(slices.Concat(merged.headers, secondary.headers))
	merged.cookies = dedupConsistentHashCookies(slices.Concat(merged.cookies, secondary.cookies))
	merged.queryParameters = dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(slices.Concat(merged.queryParameters, secondary.queryParameters))
	merged.filterState = dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(slices.Concat(merged.filterState, secondary.filterState))
	// sourceIp is a scalar and retains the primary policy's value even when unset.
	return merged
}

func mergeConsistentHash(
	p1, p2 *TrafficPolicy,
	p2Ref *ir.AttachedPolicyRef,
	p2MergeOrigins ir.MergeOrigins,
	opts policy.MergeOptions,
	mergeOrigins ir.MergeOrigins,
	tpOpts TrafficPolicyMergeOpts,
) {
	if tpOpts.ConsistentHash != "" {
		// this is merging 2 policies at the same hierarchical level (no parent-child relationship),
		// so use tpOpts since it overrides the default merge strategy
		opts.Strategy = policy.ToInternalMergeStrategy(tpOpts.ConsistentHash)
	}

	if !policy.IsMergeable(p1.spec.consistentHash, p2.spec.consistentHash, opts) {
		return
	}

	switch opts.Strategy {
	case policy.AugmentedDeepMerge, policy.OverridableDeepMerge:
		if p1.spec.consistentHash == nil {
			p1.spec.consistentHash = cloneConsistentHash(p2.spec.consistentHash)
			mergeOrigins.SetOne("consistentHash", p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins)
			return
		}
		if p2.spec.consistentHash == nil {
			return
		}

		primary, secondary := p1.spec.consistentHash, p2.spec.consistentHash
		if opts.Strategy == policy.OverridableDeepMerge {
			primary, secondary = p2.spec.consistentHash, p1.spec.consistentHash
		}

		if primary.disable {
			// A higher-priority disable suppresses all hash policies from lower-priority policies.
			p1.spec.consistentHash = &consistentHashIR{disable: true}
			if opts.Strategy == policy.OverridableDeepMerge {
				mergeOrigins.SetOne("consistentHash", p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins)
			}
			return
		}

		p1.spec.consistentHash = mergeConsistentHashIRs(primary, secondary)
		mergeOrigins.Append("consistentHash", p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins)

	default:
		accessor := fieldAccessor[consistentHashIR]{
			Get: func(spec *trafficPolicySpecIr) *consistentHashIR { return spec.consistentHash },
			Set: func(spec *trafficPolicySpecIr, val *consistentHashIR) { spec.consistentHash = val },
		}
		defaultMerge(p1, p2, p2Ref, p2MergeOrigins, opts, mergeOrigins, accessor, "consistentHash")
	}
}

+// DeepCopyInto is an autogenerated deepcopy function, copying the receiver, writing into out. in must be non-nil.
+func (in *ConsistentHashConfig) DeepCopyInto(out *ConsistentHashConfig) {
+	*out = *in
+	if in.Headers != nil {
+		in, out := &in.Headers, &out.Headers
+		*out = make([]ConsistentHashHeader, len(*in))
+		for i := range *in {
+			(*in)[i].DeepCopyInto(&(*out)[i])
+		}
+	}
+	if in.Cookies != nil {

+		*out = make([]ConsistentHashCookie, len(*in))
+		for i := range *in {
+			(*in)[i].DeepCopyInto(&(*out)[i])
+		}
+	}
+	if in.QueryParameters != nil {
+		in, out := &in.QueryParameters, &out.QueryParameters
+		*out = make([]ConsistentHashQueryParameter, len(*in))
+		for i := range *in {
+			(*in)[i].DeepCopyInto(&(*out)[i])
+		}
+	}
+	if in.FilterState != nil {
+		in, out := &in.FilterState, &out.FilterState
+		*out = make([]ConsistentHashFilterState, len(*in))
+		for i := range *in {
+			(*in)[i].DeepCopyInto(&(*out)[i])
+		}
+	}
+	if in.SourceIp != nil {
+		in, out := &in.SourceIp, &out.SourceIp
+		*out = new(ConsistentHashSourceIp)
+		(*in).DeepCopyInto(*out)
+	}
+}
+

+func (in *ConsistentHashConfig) DeepCopy() *ConsistentHashConfig {

func TestMergePoliciesConsistentHashDeepMerge(t *testing.T) {
	p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
	}}}
	p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-account"}},
		cookies: []*consistentHashCookieIR{{name: "session"}},
	}}}

	policies := []ir.PolicyAtt{
		{PolicyIr: p1, PolicyRef: &ir.AttachedPolicyRef{Name: "p1"}},
		{PolicyIr: p2, PolicyRef: &ir.AttachedPolicyRef{Name: "p2"}},
	}

	merged := policy.MergePolicies(policies, mergeTrafficPolicies, `{"trafficPolicy":{"consistentHash":"DeepMerge"}}`)
	require.NotNil(t, merged.PolicyIr)
	mergedPolicy, ok := merged.PolicyIr.(*TrafficPolicy)
	require.True(t, ok)
	require.NotNil(t, mergedPolicy.spec.consistentHash)
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"x-user", "x-account"}, headersNames(mergedPolicy.spec.consistentHash.headers))
	require.Len(t, mergedPolicy.spec.consistentHash.cookies, 1)
	assert.Contains(t, merged.MergeOrigins["consistentHash"], "p2")
}

func TestConsistentHashIREquals(t *testing.T) {
	a := &consistentHashIR{
		disable: false,
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user", terminal: true}},
		cookies: []*consistentHashCookieIR{{name: "session", ttl: durationPtr(time.Hour)}},
		sourceIp: &consistentHashSourceIpIR{terminal: true},
	}
	b := &consistentHashIR{
		disable: false,
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user", terminal: true}},
		cookies: []*consistentHashCookieIR{{name: "session", ttl: durationPtr(time.Hour)}},
		sourceIp: &consistentHashSourceIpIR{terminal: true},
	}
	assert.True(t, a.Equals(b))

	b.headers[0].terminal = false
	assert.False(t, a.Equals(b))
	assert.False(t, a.Equals(nil))
}

func TestConsistentHashIRValidate(t *testing.T) {
	valid := &consistentHashIR{
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{
			headerName: "x-user",
			regexRewrite: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute{
				Pattern: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatcher{Regex: "^foo(.*)$"},
			},
		}},
	}
	require.NoError(t, valid.Validate())

	invalid := &consistentHashIR{
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{
			headerName: "x-user",
			regexRewrite: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatchAndSubstitute{
				Pattern: &envoy_type_matcher_v3.RegexMatcher{Regex: "[invalid("},
			},
		}},
	}
	require.Error(t, invalid.Validate())
	assert.Contains(t, invalid.Validate().Error(), "invalid regex pattern")
}

// ConsistentHashConfig configures consistent hashing for a route.
// When set, RouteAction hash policies are produced in the following canonical order:
// headers, cookies, queryParameters, filterState, and finally sourceIp.
// If no sub-fields are set, a single sourceIp hash policy is used.
// +kubebuilder:validation:XValidation:rule="self.disable ? (!has(self.headers) && !has(self.cookies) && !has(self.queryParameters) && !has(self.filterState) && !has(self.sourceIp)) : true",message="when disable is true, no other fields may be set"
type ConsistentHashConfig struct {
	// Disable suppresses consistent hashing on the route.
	// When true, no hash policies are produced and any hash policies inherited from
	// broader-scoped policies are suppressed.
	// +optional
	Disable bool `json:"disable,omitempty"`

	// Headers is a list of request headers to use as components of the hash key.
	// +optional
	Headers []ConsistentHashHeader `json:"headers,omitempty"`

	// Cookies is a list of cookies to use as components of the hash key.
	// +optional
	Cookies []ConsistentHashCookie `json:"cookies,omitempty"`

	// QueryParameters is a list of query parameters to use as components of the hash key.
	// +optional
	QueryParameters []ConsistentHashQueryParameter `json:"queryParameters,omitempty"`

	// FilterState is a list of per-request filter state objects to use as components of the hash key.
	// +optional
	FilterState []ConsistentHashFilterState `json:"filterState,omitempty"`

	// SourceIp uses the request source IP address as a component of the hash key.
	// +optional
	SourceIp *ConsistentHashSourceIp `json:"sourceIp,omitempty"`
}

// ConsistentHashHeader configures a header to be used as a component of the hash key.
type ConsistentHashHeader struct {
	// HeaderName is the name of the request header to use to obtain the hash key.
	// If the request header is not present, no hash is produced.
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	HeaderName string `json:"headerName"`

	// RegexRewrite, if specified, rewrites the request header value before hashing.
	// +optional
	RegexRewrite *ConsistentHashRegexRewrite `json:"regexRewrite,omitempty"`

	// Terminal, if true, stops hash computation after this policy if a hash key is available.
	// +optional
	Terminal *bool `json:"terminal,omitempty"`
}

// ConsistentHashRegexRewrite rewrites a header value before it is used as a hash key.
type ConsistentHashRegexRewrite struct {
	// Pattern is the regular expression to match against the header value.
	// The pattern must be a valid RE2 regular expression.
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	Pattern string `json:"pattern"`

	// Substitution is the replacement string for the matched pattern.
	// It can include backreferences to captured groups from the pattern (e.g., \1, \2)
	// or named groups (e.g., \g<name>).
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	Substitution string `json:"substitution"`
}

// ConsistentHashCookie configures a cookie to be used as a component of the hash key.
type ConsistentHashCookie struct {
	// Name is the name of the cookie to use to obtain the hash key.
	// If the cookie is not present and ttl is not set, no hash is produced.
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	Name string `json:"name"`

	// TTL specifies the time to live of the cookie.
	// It accepts a Go duration string (e.g. "1h30m") or plain integer seconds (e.g. "3600").
	// If specified, Envoy generates a cookie with the given TTL if the cookie is not present.
	// +optional
	TTL *string `json:"ttl,omitempty"`

	// Path is the name of the path for the cookie.
	// If unset, no path is set for the generated cookie.
	// +optional
	Path *string `json:"path,omitempty"`

	// Attributes are additional cookie attributes (for example SameSite, Secure) passed through
	// to Envoy as-is. They are used when Envoy generates a new cookie.
	// +optional
	Attributes []ConsistentHashCookieAttribute `json:"attributes,omitempty"`

	// Terminal, if true, stops hash computation after this policy if a hash key is available.
	// +optional
	Terminal *bool `json:"terminal,omitempty"`
}

// ConsistentHashCookieAttribute is a name/value pair for an additional cookie attribute.
type ConsistentHashCookieAttribute struct {
	// Name is the name of the cookie attribute.
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	Name string `json:"name"`

	// Value is the optional value of the cookie attribute.
	// +optional
	Value string `json:"value,omitempty"`
}

// ConsistentHashQueryParameter configures a query parameter to be used as a component of the hash key.
type ConsistentHashQueryParameter struct {
	// Name is the name of the URL query parameter to use to obtain the hash key.
	// If the parameter is not present, no hash is produced.
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	Name string `json:"name"`

	// Terminal, if true, stops hash computation after this policy if a hash key is available.
	// +optional
	Terminal *bool `json:"terminal,omitempty"`
}

// ConsistentHashFilterState configures a per-request filter state object to be used as a component of the hash key.
type ConsistentHashFilterState struct {
	// Key is the name of the object in the per-request filter state.
	// If there is no data associated with the key, no hash is produced.
	// +kubebuilder:validation:MinLength=1
	// +required
	Key string `json:"key"`

	// Terminal, if true, stops hash computation after this policy if a hash key is available.
	// +optional
	Terminal *bool `json:"terminal,omitempty"`
}

// ConsistentHashSourceIp configures the request source IP address to be used as a component of the hash key.
type ConsistentHashSourceIp struct {
	// Terminal, if true, stops hash computation after this policy if a hash key is available.
	// +optional
	Terminal *bool `json:"terminal,omitempty"`
}

func TestMergeConsistentHashSourceIpRetainsHigherPriorityEvenWhenUnset(t *testing.T) {
	p1 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
		headers: []*consistentHashHeaderIR{{headerName: "x-user"}},
	}}}
	p2 := &TrafficPolicy{spec: trafficPolicySpecIr{consistentHash: &consistentHashIR{
		sourceIp: &consistentHashSourceIpIR{terminal: true},
	}}}
	mergeConsistentHash(p1, p2, &ir.AttachedPolicyRef{Name: "p2"}, nil, policy.MergeOptions{Strategy: policy.AugmentedDeepMerge}, ir.MergeOrigins{}, TrafficPolicyMergeOpts{})
	require.NotNil(t, p1.spec.consistentHash)
	assert.Nil(t, p1.spec.consistentHash.sourceIp)
}

func dedupConsistentHashHeaders(headers []*consistentHashHeaderIR) []*consistentHashHeaderIR {
	if len(headers) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(headers))
	out := make([]*consistentHashHeaderIR, 0, len(headers))
	for _, header := range headers {
		if header == nil {
			continue
		}
		key := strings.ToLower(header.headerName)
		if _, ok := seen[key]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[key] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, header)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashCookies(cookies []*consistentHashCookieIR) []*consistentHashCookieIR {
	if len(cookies) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(cookies))
	out := make([]*consistentHashCookieIR, 0, len(cookies))
	for _, cookie := range cookies {
		if cookie == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[cookie.name]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[cookie.name] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, cookie)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashQueryParameters(params []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR) []*consistentHashQueryParameterIR {
	if len(params) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(params))
	out := make([]*consistentHashQueryParameterIR, 0, len(params))
	for _, param := range params {
		if param == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[param.name]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[param.name] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, param)
	}
	return out
}

func dedupConsistentHashFilterStates(states []*consistentHashFilterStateIR) []*consistentHashFilterStateIR {
	if len(states) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(states))
	out := make([]*consistentHashFilterStateIR, 0, len(states))
	for _, state := range states {
		if state == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, ok := seen[state.key]; ok {
			continue
		}
		seen[state.key] = struct{}{}
		out = append(out, state)
	}
	return out
}

// applyConsistentHash applies the consistent hash configuration to the Envoy route.
func applyConsistentHash(consistentHash *consistentHashIR, out *envoyroutev3.Route) {
	if consistentHash == nil || out == nil {
		return
	}
	action := out.GetRoute()
	if action == nil {
		return
	}


STEP 153 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && sed -n '470,500p' pkg/kgateway/extensions2/plugins/trafficpolicy/consistent_hash.go
OUTPUT:
			Terminal: consistentHash.sourceIp.terminal,
		})
	}

	// If no hash policies were specified, default to a single sourceIp hash policy.
	if len(policies) == 0 {
		policies = append(policies, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy{
			PolicySpecifier: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties_{
				ConnectionProperties: &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_ConnectionProperties{
					SourceIp: true,
				},
			},
		})
	}

	return policies
}

func buildCookieAttributes(attributes []consistentHashCookieAttributeIR) []*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute {
	if len(attributes) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	out := make([]*envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute, 0, len(attributes))
	for _, attribute := range attributes {
		out = append(out, &envoyroutev3.RouteAction_HashPolicy_CookieAttribute{
			Name:  attribute.name,
			Value: attribute.value,
		})
	}
	return out
}
